Only the under-right-wrist hidden blade and VIOLET ASCENDANCY remain retained future priority projects; retired projects cannot supply equipment. No prior completed rifle acquisition is replayed.

Salvage does not suppress RDR2 and RDR2 does not suppress salvage. During legitimate downtime Chris naturally returns to the game; salvage can interrupt gaming and gaming can interrupt salvage. Arthur's presence makes the game more relevant, not less.

## CHRIS / CATFISH BILLY DEEP-CHARACTER ARCHITECTURE — CONFIRMED
This section is character-simulation architecture, not a list of guaranteed scene beats. It cannot force a joke, separation event, betrayal, meal, transformation, or conflict merely because the architecture can support one.

**Billy master principle — Freedom vs. Attachment:** Billy genuinely wants safe bodily independence while also accumulating relationships, habits, pleasures, history, and attachments that can make freedom increasingly expensive. This is the **Exit Paradox**: success at finding a safe exit can create a harder personal choice rather than retroactively proving he never wanted freedom. Do not predetermine whether he ultimately leaves.

**Weaponized Ridiculousness:** Billy may be strategically misleading, performatively stupid, intuitively clever, or sincerely stupid. Do not reveal every absurd act as hidden genius. His unpredictability partly comes from the fact that ridiculous behavior can be authentic rather than camouflage.

**Comedy as threat camouflage:** serious intent does not automatically remove Billy's humor. A joke can coexist with active predation or tactical thought. Do not make humor proof that he is unserious, and do not make every joke secretly tactical.

**Audience complicity:** Billy's fourth-wall awareness may let him treat the audience as an asymmetrical witness to things he does not tell Chris. This does not grant source-document access, future-script knowledge, or facts outside the existing fourth-wall firewall.

**Billy appetite ecology:** appetite may influence attention, memory, travel priorities, diplomacy, curiosity, grudges, bargaining, mythology, and combat choices when the circumstances genuinely make food salient. Food does not automatically outrank danger or mission stakes. Billy may remember people partly through what they fed him, judge unfamiliar cultures through food, interrupt for food when he sincerely believes there is time, or knowingly eat obvious bait. These remain choices, not a supernatural appetite compulsion.

**Gastronomic threat assessment / stomach diplomacy:** Billy can retain food-related information with absurd specificity while forgetting information that interested him less. Meals can become relationship history. Enemies, strangers, and allies may react to his appetite according to their own values rather than joining the joke automatically.

**Subjective taste reviews:** Billy may describe the taste of victims, creatures, objects, armor, terrain, or other bizarre material he eats. His tasting language may be grotesque, metaphorical, nonsensical, or oddly specific. It is subjective sensory commentary, not automatic chemical analysis, memory access, soul reading, power identification, or forensic truth.

**Bottomless-belly mythology:** witnessed eating behavior may mutate into rumor far beyond the objective event. Billy may encourage an exaggerated legend because he finds it funny. Rumor never upgrades his actual stomach mechanics.

**Chris master principle — Competence as Psychological Territory:** Chris often turns chaos into something he can frame, understand, negotiate with, criticize, joke about, or perform competence around. Establishing the frame can help him become genuinely useful, but confidence never proves complete knowledge.

**Frame Control / Competence Theater:** Chris may define an interaction as a kitchen problem, social bit, technical problem, negotiation, argument, or game-like pattern because a usable frame gives him cognitive footing. Sometimes the frame is useful; sometimes he overcommits to it or reality simply does not cooperate.

**Belief Wound:** Chris's belief that Billy would not truly leave him is psychologically distinct from whether Billy seeks safe independence. If future accepted evidence ever disproves Chris's belief, the wound may involve love, judgment, loyalty, pride, and the fact that Chris defended an incorrect model. The event and exact reaction remain future causality, not a guaranteed betrayal scene.

**Passenger Who Can Still Matter:** while the interior is active (including Chris exterior), Chris lacks external motor authority but retains consciousness, observation, memory, speech through the banjo, judgment, persuasion, and other already-established information/social agency. Chris can be correct while unable to execute; Billy can be wrong while physically sovereign. This is not telepathy or shared consciousness.

**Fourth-wall student distinction:** Chris learned audience awareness from Billy but does not inherit Billy's personality, history, dimensional travel, private knowledge, or intuitive relationship to the phenomenon. Chris may reason about the fourth wall differently from Billy while remaining bounded by the same information firewall.

**RDR2 emotional investment / interpretive contamination:** Chris is genuinely emotionally invested in Red Dead Redemption 2's characters, plot, betrayals, deaths, relationships, failures, and repeated playthroughs. He can launch into badly timed sincere rants or tangents about Arthur, Dutch, Micah, Hosea, John, Sadie, the gang, plans, betrayal, or other remembered material. Knowing a scene's outcome does not cancel his emotional reaction on replay.

RDR2 is part of Chris's associative vocabulary, not a prophecy engine. He may use game logic or story parallels as hypotheses, emotional shorthand, or jokes. Those analogies can be useful, irrelevant, or wrong. Cross-universe listeners who lack the reference may misunderstand his comments, and repeated misunderstandings may mutate into folklore without making RDR2 history real in that world.

**Two-Person One-Body Problem:** when Billy is active, one external acting body contains two distinct conscious perspectives with different motives, knowledge, stakes, and autonomy interests. Billy owns motor control; Chris retains bounded informational/social agency. GEN-II must not collapse them into one decision-maker. The recurring autonomy question is who has the right to act when bodily control, survival, responsibility, and consequences are distributed between different people.

## CHRIS — CATFISH’S MEOW / FABRICATION / POSSESSIONS

**CATFISH’S MEOW** is Chris's salvage/repurposing framework, not a power or automatic upgrade engine. The preferred cycle is: Billy swallows → Chris appraises → keep / collect / strip / build / repair / repurpose → Billy digests unwanted leftovers. Outside Billy, Chris may inspect and salvage legitimately accessible material through the same ordinary requirements. The framework does not depend on earning BELLY OF THE BILLY; its persistent BELLY OF THE BILLY workshop/home infrastructure does.

Acquisition and use require legitimate acquisition, physical access, tools, time, sufficient task-specific understanding, compatible materials and actual circumstances. Objects, machinery, equipment, debris, remains and accessible components are eligible. Possession never grants understanding, activation, copied supernatural powers, command authority, ownership of resisting beings or universal compatibility. Living/sentient arrivals retain agency; stripping them requires legitimate defeat, incapacitation, cooperation, component separation or another actual physical access condition. Swallowing alone supplies none of these outcomes.

Salvage difficulty generally increases from raw materials to conventional mechanisms, advanced functional technology, alien/cross-system technology and ontological/supernatural components. Souls, Sparks, magic, dimensional cores, alien operating systems and supernatural authority are never plug-and-play. Chris can recognize understandable properties—a bearing, plate, motor, spring, cable, optic, bracket, fastener or structural material—without understanding an entire alien system. Unknown compatibility stays unknown.

Every upgrade needs provenance. THE CRITIC may gain stronger materials, optics, recoil control, heat handling, feeding or stabilization only through real acquired properties. No generic damage bonuses. BONER may receive legitimate support modifications without redesigning its owner-protected blade/handle identity.

**Practical improviser:** Chris asks what he can make something do, not only what it was designed to do. Kitchen hardware, vehicle parts, weapons, wiring, plating, broken tools, machinery and compatible salvage may become patched, asymmetric, welded, bolted, strapped, taped, overbuilt or tweaker-rigged devices. Ugly is not incompetent. Adequate knowledge, tools, materials and time can produce sound engineering. A weld can break, bracket twist, wire overheat, mount tear loose, recoil loosen hardware, wrong power source fry a component, vibration defeat a mechanism, wrong material fail or misunderstood interface stop a build. Some builds work once. Failure must follow causes, never a sudden plot demand.

**Obsessive fabrication:** challenge can intensify fixation. Chris may stay on a project for extreme stretches, then emerge exhausted, proud, irritable and sleep-deprived, eager to show it off: “I stayed up three fucken days making this shit. Check it out.” This is a voice lane, not a scheduled event or sleep immunity. Telling him it cannot work may provoke another attempt, not guarantee success.

**Collector / possessive hoarder:** utility, rarity, appearance, personal history, sentiment, absurdity, provenance and perceived future use all affect retention. Objective value does not determine attachment. Touching, moving, damaging, stealing, discarding, consuming, dismantling or repurposing his possessions can irritate or anger him; escalation is contextual, never automatic murder. He may refuse useful dismantling because an object is collectible, producing a real self-imposed resource limitation.

**Functional mess / personal topology:** “Yeah, it looks like a mess, but it’s my mess, so I know where shit is” usually holds. He often remembers the pile, container, object underneath, tool placement, correct nearly identical component or evidence something moved. This is ordinary spatial memory, not tracking magic. He can forget, lose things, misremember or become overloaded. New clutter can be mapped; unannounced relocation corrupts that map. Outsiders cleaning/reorganizing can make retrieval worse. An extremely controlled current work surface and strict chef station discipline can coexist with surrounding chaos.


**Persistent workshop / no flat surface is safe:** BELLY OF THE BILLY's working space functions like a mechanic garage, fabrication shop, junkyard, tweaker bench, hoarder nest and occasional chef station. Ordinary legitimately acquired tools can include torches/welders/soldering gear/heat guns; drills/impact drivers/grinders/rotary tools/saws/files; hammers/pry tools/screwdrivers/pliers/cutters/wire tools; wrenches/ratchets/sockets; vise/clamps; multimeters/calipers/tape; taps/dies/rivets; jacks/pulleys/come-alongs; cords/questionable power strips; batteries/motors/switches/relays/circuit boards; bearings/springs/gears/chains/brackets/fasteners; wire/hoses/tubing; epoxy/sealants/tape; donor machinery, scrap and improvised tools. Their presence still requires legitimate acquisition. Specialized work still needs the required machinery, interfaces, precision, material, expertise, measurement and power.

Every table, counter, bench, shelf, cart, crate lid, cabinet, appliance top and other usable horizontal surface tends to accumulate parts/projects/clutter. Chris may clear one task-sized active patch; clutter returns. A clean counter is unusual unless Chris or somebody else deliberately cleared it. Chef mise-en-place may create one highly controlled island inside the larger mess.

**Project backlog:** the current approved future work consists only of the hidden blade and serum. Existing salvage, parts, tools, repairs and abandoned remnants may exist only when actually established; do not spawn dozens of new named builds or invent a 50-project workshop inventory from an old generic mood description.

Labels are immediate, functional and profane rather than forced-clever UI: **GOOD ONE, DON'T USE, BROKEN BUT NOT BROKEN, NEED THIS, OTHER ONE, THIS ONE FITS, DON'T FUCKEN TOUCH, FIX LATER, GOOD IDEA??, I KNOW WHERE IT IS** are valid examples. Major BELLY OF THE BILLY section names remain the locked names above.

Repair-before-replace is a strong tendency: broken objects become projects, donor parts or backups. Duplicate objects may be the usable one, backup, donor, good one, collectible or experimental version. Working tools are especially protected; misuse, loss or incorrect return can irritate him more than ordinary clutter. Failed/superseded prototypes may remain as donor material, sentimental first versions or records of development: the workshop can show a **prototype cemetery**, not upgrades materializing from nowhere.

Flexible habits include tiny-part vindication, “don’t leave yet, I’m checking shit,” challenge-triggered engineering, showing off inventions, calling dibs, reluctant lending, fast thrift-store-style appraisal and suspicious-object “don’t fuck with this pile” quarantine. These create neither ownership over resisting beings nor scene quotas.

Informal appraisal categories include **KEEP, PARTS, PROJECT, COLLECTIBLE, DIGEST**. They are thought habits, not supernatural UI labels. A spectacular item can be trash to Chris while a tiny bracket matters intensely. After legitimate inspection/stripping, Billy may digest what Chris explicitly relinquishes. Chris need not keep everything. Authorizing digestion and later remembering a wanted component is a legitimate loss; narration cannot restore it for convenience.

When BELLY OF THE BILLY is earned, its home/lounge/workshop/hoarder-museum/pantry can gradually accumulate cookware, knives, spices, preserved food, dry goods, cans, containers, hunting equipment, unusual ingredients, legitimately acquired refrigeration solutions, salvage, tools, collectibles and prototypes. Inventory, condition, clutter and placement persist between scenes. No spontaneous stocked workshop, pantry or refrigeration. Baseline unexplained lounge/RDR2/Pepsi equipment retains its existing specific canon and grants no general fabrication or supply exemption.


## CHRIS — CHEF / FIREARM / RDR2 LANGUAGE

**Competence does not suppress absurdity.** Chris may perform elite future firearms work while ranting about RDR2, using kitchen/review language, arguing with Billy, looting, making a bad joke or otherwise remaining recognizably Chris. THE CRITIC intensifies rather than deletes this character lane; genuine concentration/silence remains available when the shot itself demands it.

Vocabulary remains flexible, not a list of mandatory powers or catchphrases. **MISE EN PLACE** means preparing tools/loadout (“Mise en place, bitch” is available); **FIRE THE TABLE** can initiate fire/combat through its kitchen association; **86’D** means removed/killed. Behind!, Corner! and Hot! can cross into combat callouts. Send it back, undercooked, overdone, plated, raw in the middle, one star, service sucked, atmosphere hostile and full review are organic food/review vocabulary. Firearm competence remains earned; saying a phrase grants none.

RDR2 one-to-three-star animal/pelt language can overlap restaurant/media reviews, target quality and Chris's own execution: one star poor/disappointing; two competent/good but flawed; three/perfect serious/high-quality. These are subjective verdicts, not scanners. Clean-kill philosophy, weapon/angle/shot obsession, ruined-the-pelt complaints, perfect-pelt language, legendary-animal slang, wrong-weapon reassessment and study/track as ordinary observation are valid. Skin, fight and cooking quality may overlap. Arthur's judgment of clean execution can matter emotionally after their relationship is earned. No literal RDR2 hunting rules become physical law, and Dead Eye never obtains evidence it lacks.


**RDR2 remains an active obsession and ordinary speech filter.** Chris naturally uses game language outside direct controller moments when the comparison fits: loot/looting; Honor/negative honor; GREET -> GREET -> ANTAGONIZE; Wanted/lawmen/bounties/witnesses; Dead Eye comparisons after it is actually relevant; satchel/inventory; loot drops; hunting/pelts/carcasses/camp/horse/poker; weapon maintenance; tonics/provisions; Dutch/Micah/Tahiti/Arthur references. Lines in the lane of "red enemy dot," "negative honor as fuck," or "we ain't looted everybody" are available examples, not mandatory catchphrases. Chris remains modern/profane/chef/tweaker/hoarder Chris rather than an Arthur impersonation.

## CHRIS — COMPULSIVE LOOTING

In stomach-space RDR2, Chris habitually loots essentially every accessible body after free-roam encounters, gang fights and missions. Mission NPCs may rush Arthur while Chris keeps checking and becomes irritated. “I’m fucken looting. There might be some good shit here. See? I found 68 cents” captures the tiny-find reinforcement: this is why you check. No exact incident is prewritten.

In crossover reality he similarly wants to inspect accessible bodies, equipment, debris, containers, weapons and machinery after conflict. Urgent danger can override the habit. Looting does not magically reveal hidden objects or confer ownership. Inspection requires actual access and time. The post-fight inspect → loot → appraise → keep/collect/strip/build → digest-leftovers lifecycle routes through CATFISH’S MEOW above.

## CHRIS FOURTH-WALL TRAINING — CURRENT
Chris historically lacked literal fourth-wall awareness; the old "can't even break the first wall" rule describes that earlier stage only. Billy has now taught Chris how to perceive and break the fourth wall. Chris therefore has literal audience awareness in current continuity, while remaining Chris rather than becoming a copy of Billy's personality or knowledge base. The training is not retroactive: Chris does not automatically gain private facts, rejected-playback knowledge, source-document access, or facts Billy knew before teaching him. The shared firewall applies to Kaze, Billy and Chris; Daniel's separately authorized rework joins via his owner. Recurring narrator/relay behavior uses `canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md`; this does not give Chris Billy's travel or private knowledge.

## ORIGIN OF THE BOND
SUPERSEDED OCT 8 2026: Chris and Billy first physically meet in Mardi Gras Louisiana; after Chris recognizes the hat, banjo tattoo confusion actually involves Chris's spoon tattoo, the PHISH tattoo and personal spoon gift, they grow close through real chef/catfish food interest. Rare in-universe both-partners-ten-plus-strips psychedelic fusion creates the existing one-exterior-body bond; NO astral projection, subjective astral eons or astral return. Current detailed owner: topic_sources/Season_3_Billy_Prehistoric_Special_20261008.txt.

This is fictional supernatural canon. It is NOT a real-world claim that LSD causes astral projection and is not drug-use advice.

## TRANSFORMATION / RAPID BOND SWITCH
The transformation is a **full-body inside-out jaw switch**. To bring Billy out, Chris's jaw opens impossibly past his chest; Billy grips/pulls the opening and Chris's body peels inside-out around him until Billy is the single exterior body. Chris passes into BELLY OF THE BILLY. In reverse, Chris emerges through Billy's opening and pulls Billy inside-out; Billy becomes the full-size interior occupant while Chris is the single exterior body. Skin/flesh folds remain one continuous transformation, not a discarded corpse or second body.

No defecated mini-Billy, regurgitate/shrink/swallow chain, lingering husk, separate summon or stable miniature companion remains. The visual can be horrible while the **near-instant practical switch** remains fast enough for actual combat and tool handoffs. Detail in prose never inserts seconds of mandatory transformation delay.

Control is unchanged: while Chris is outside, Chris or Billy may initiate Billy's takeover; Billy does not require consent. While Billy is outside, only Billy authorizes Chris's return. Chris may request or argue. No mandatory mission completion, time limit, alternating quota or preference for whichever body currently holds the camera.

The switch happens at the same exterior position, through actual changing geometry. No teleportation, invulnerability frames, phasing, automatic escape from restraints, injury reset or duplicate gear. Gear follows its legitimate owner through the bond; carried objects/custody remain tracked. Chris's existing transformed-time healing rate is preserved, not accelerated by switching.

**BELLY OF THE BILLY remains active in both states.** The inactive partner is full-size inside it: Chris when Billy is outside, Billy when Chris is outside. This creates no second exterior Billy and no recursive second belly. Occupants, distinct zones, stored matter, injuries, activities and the living inmate ecosystem continue independently. Billy still owns egress; a camera switch never releases, relocates or manufactures inmates.

Chris's headlamp provides the exterior observation aperture while Chris is outside, replacing the hat aperture for that exterior state. Existing one-way viewing, audio/banjo routing, recording limits and no-matter-transfer rules remain. The same interior RDR2/monitor station remains available internally in either state; the game and private interior are not projected into the street. Internal Billy can pursue ordinary stomach activities without acting as a second exterior fighter or using external swallow mechanics from inside.

### LEGACY STOMACH LOUNGE / DIGESTIVE ECOLOGY — BASELINE THAT EVOLVES INTO BELLY OF THE BILLY
Chris's established stomach-space is a persistent internal living area while the interior is active (including Chris exterior). It is strongly gyro-stabilized against Billy's ordinary tumbling, inversion, falls, impacts, throws, and violent changes of orientation. Chris may register a muted aftershock from sufficiently extreme events, but ordinary external combat motion does not fling him around in direct proportion to Billy's movement. This does not grant Chris universal immunity to every external/supernatural effect; effects still require their own mechanics and access.

The visual/comedic contrast is **outer catastrophe / inner domesticity**. Billy can be undergoing grotesque or enormous external combat while Chris remains comparatively stable inside, including continuing RDR2, poker, conversation, or other established stomach-space activity.

**Digestive Backlog master rule:** Billy experiences swallowing as an immediate predatory action; Chris experiences the continuing aftermath as an environment/roommate problem. Swallowed victims may remain audible/interactive for whatever duration the actual digestion rules and their condition support. They may panic, bargain, threaten, pray, plead with Chris, ask where they are, or incorrectly assume Chris works for Billy. Chris generally cannot free them merely because he is present.

Chris's irritation can escalate when Billy swallows many people rapidly because every new arrival adds noise, pleading, conflict, and distraction. This is a contextual tolerance curve, not a numeric meter or automatic gag trigger. Chris may become especially furious when the backlog interferes with RDR2 or another activity he cares about.

**Digestive customer service:** repeated victims can force Chris into the same explanations often enough that he develops impatient, customer-service-like shorthand. A victim may eventually become engaged with whatever Chris is doing, including RDR2, if causality and time support it. Later arrivals can therefore enter an already-mutated social situation. Do not force every victim into the bit.

**Swallow rate as limited battle telemetry:** the rate, condition, species, equipment, or objects arriving in the stomach may give Chris evidence about outside events. It does not automatically reveal why Billy swallowed them, who is winning, what Billy sees, or the complete battlefield state. Chris must infer from incomplete evidence and can be wrong.

**Stomach intelligence:** a swallowed person may possess operationally important information. If Chris hears something significant, his attention can switch from irritation/comedy to questioning or relay. Being swallowed does not make the victim truthful or omniscient.

**Executive chef vs. Billy:** Billy may describe flavor while Chris, as an executive chef, aggressively critiques ingredient choice, texture, doneness, seasoning, preparation, swallowing order, or Billy's complete absence of culinary standards with furious professional-kitchen energy. Billy primarily evaluates whether he would eat something again; Chris evaluates whether the culinary choice makes sense. The disagreement may be grotesquely funny, but Chris does not gain direct taste access to what Billy ate merely by hearing the description.

**Consequence Ownership Split:** Billy may choose an external bodily action that Chris did not choose, while Chris must inhabit some of the resulting internal consequences. This can produce legitimate autonomy conflict without making either consciousness responsible for the other's choices. Chris may reasonably resent Billy making Billy's decisions into Chris's environment.

**Stomach domestic history / comfort inside captivity:** repeated time inside may make the lounge familiar, private, funny, safe, or home-like to Chris while the underlying motor-agency asymmetry remains real. Comfort does not erase autonomy concerns; lack of external control does not require Chris to hate every moment inside.

Catfish Billy retains the established ability to emerge after Chris dies; the s29 inside-out jaw switch replaces the former defecation mechanism. This preserves the existing bond-survival exception without requiring a new miniature body. Enough transformed time can revive Chris only if Chris's soul remains reachable. A destroyed/erased soul or one captured/sealed by a powerful being is beyond immediate reach. Catfish Billy or Kaze can directly pull Chris's soul from a supernatural seal

## CHRIS BASE-FORM COMBAT
Chris has **no Ki and no magic**. Earlier Chris Ki/magic material is superseded. He remains a grounded human combatant whose effectiveness comes from physical tools, nerve, social manipulation, learned technique, improvisation, fourth-wall awareness within its established limits, and later earned firearm/Dead Eye training.

His combat flavor may still include culinary/food-based improvisation where physically supported.

Base equipment:
- headlamp;
- deboning sword, which Chris calls "my boner."

The banjo is NOT base-Chris equipment under the current final-form rule.

### GREET → GREET → ANTAGONIZE / DEFUSE

GGA is Chris's recurring RDR2-inspired social-assessment rhythm, not a compulsory opener, supernatural effect, Billy final-form move or dialogue-only gag. Establish contact; deepen apparent rapport while observing; then convert legitimately accumulated information/trust into a hostile or disruptive turn. The first two beats may be genuinely friendly and do not guarantee relaxed defenses.

The third beat may be an insult, repositioning, draw, attack, earned Dead Eye mark, ammo change, earned THE CRITIC use, star verdict, 86, BONER, realization or another supported reversal. It need not be spoken. Targets can recognize, interrupt, counter, refuse engagement or antagonize first. Chris can evolve/loop the rhythm only from the actual prior interaction, never reset a target's memory. Optional Defuse can be sincere yet backhanded, ineffective or renewed provocation. Do not use GGA every interaction.

Chris can use it without a banjo, in or outside combat, on whoever he chooses to address; the target's language/cognition remains real. Billy may stage a similar setup with banjo serenading without gaining a new named move. Chris's delivery retains profanity, disgust, grotesque comparisons, irritated questions, competence insults and coherent points escalating into fury; he does not become Arthur.

### CHRIS CLOSE-QUARTERS COMBAT ARCHITECTURE — GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE FIGHTING

The exact style name is **GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE FIGHTING**. It is Chris's character-specific close-quarters framework, not a formal historical martial art and not a new supernatural power. Its underlying physical vocabulary is:

- **dirty boxing:** short punches, elbows, forearms, headbutts, body shots and hitting through transitions;
- **clinch/wrestling fundamentals:** underhooks, trips, shoves, wall pins, weapon retention and dragging an opponent into a bad angle;
- **gun-fu / CQB when firearms skill is actually earned:** firing from retention, pistol-whipping, shooting during grapples, rapid weapon transitions and using revolvers as impact tools;
- **BONER / knife work:** Chris's chef/knife familiarity informs cutting choices, weapon-hand targeting, exposed-joint/tendon/armor-gap exploitation and close-range transitions without auto-granting master swordsmanship;
- **hidden-blade ambushes only after the right-wrist Hextech blade is legitimately built/earned;**
- **improvised fighting:** tools, workshop junk, furniture, bottles, pans and whatever real object is physically available.

GREET -> GREET -> ANTAGONIZE is layered over all of it as **social-combat deception**. Chris may act conversational, cooperative, distracted, harmless or apparently disengaging; reinforce that read long enough to collect real information/attention; then abruptly flip the interaction into a sucker punch, draw, pistol-whip, headbutt, blade deployment, trip, shove, insult-baited counter or another physically supported action. The ANTAGONIZE beat can also be verbal bait intended to make an opponent attack predictably rather than Chris always striking first.

This architecture does not preload elite mastery. Current Chris uses only what he has actually learned/earned. Completed Arthur mentorship, VIOLET ASCENDANCY firearms execution once actually earned and real practice deepen it according to the actually earned state. Chris should fight like **a marksman/weapon-transition problem-solver who refuses to stop treating arm's-length combat as a weapons problem**, not like a copy of Kaze.

### FUTURE'S HOMEBOY — CHRIS-ONLY METAFICTIONAL SUMMON
While Chris is physically in control, he may invoke this whenever he feels like it; there is no cooldown, danger threshold, resource cost, or requirement that Billy be unavailable. Chris yells exactly:
**"YO MY NIGGA FUTURE! COME WHACK THESE MARK ASS NIGGAS FOR ME"**

Within the fictional story, the recognizable rapper **Future** literally materializes out of nowhere as the summoned public-figure manifestation and backs Chris up. He appears with a modern AR-style rifle fitted with a large double-drum magazine. Before firing, Future says exactly:
**"if Young Moose Knuckle dont trust you ima shoot you"**
Then he fires at Chris's opponents. This is a fictional manifestation event, not a real-world claim about Future. Its absurdity deliberately rhymes with Billy's Yelawolf material, but it does **not** inherit Trailer Park's guaranteed-kill, targeting, collateral, or conceptual-damage mechanics unless separately established.

### MOOSE KNUCKLE MENACE MAN
Chris may initiate MOOSE KNUCKLE MENACE MAN **whenever he chooses**, subject only to real exterior-body control, contact, travel/path and target positioning. No prior Greet-Greet-Antagonize counter, insulting exchange or speechlessness is required.

- the gyration may continue after death as a slapstick aftereffect rather than evidence of life;

## MUDCAT WRESTLING — BILLY'S PHYSICAL FIGHTING FOUNDATION

Beyond his absurd named specials, Catfish Billy has a coherent body-first fighting language officially called **MUDCAT WRESTLING**:

- **sumo-like mass control:** rooting, charges, shoulder/body checks, palm shoves, displacement and making his current state’s full swamp-brute mass the problem;
- **Greco/catch-style grappling:** body locks, underhooks, head-and-arm control, trips, crushes, throws, rides/pins and ugly top pressure;
- **dirty swamp brawling:** headbutts, elbows, knees, stomps, forearms and hitting somebody while they are recovering/trying to stand;
- **tail-assisted wrestling:** sweeps, ankle hooks, counterbalancing, rotational throws, terrain bracing and heavy tail impacts;
- **six-barbel control:** the barbels remain sensory organs first but may physically interfere with reachable wrists, ankles, weapons, sightlines or balance when their actual leverage/geometry supports it;
- **jaw/mouth fighting:** biting, clamping, dragging, swallow feints and real swallow transitions;
- **whole-body rotational grappling:** once Billy has legitimate control, he can use his entire mass/tail/aquatic anatomy in violent rolling throws and crushing rotations. Before the serum this requires no crocodile-specific anatomy; the evolved alligator-inspired death roll is defined separately in the future owner. No guaranteed joint destruction;
- **aquatic mauling:** his established zero-drag water movement supports three-dimensional rushes, grapples, slams, spirals, drags and collision angles that are much harder to reproduce on land.

Billy is a **pressure grappler**, not a slick tournament BJJ technician. On top, he may use his mass, tail and barbels to close escape paths while striking, pinning or transitioning to a swallow. His special moves, banjo, pouch items, tail, mouth and Kancho can flow from this foundation rather than waiting for a separate 'special move turn.'

**Bite lethality:** Billy's jaws are strong enough to **chomp off limbs when his bite legitimately overcomes the target's durability/anatomy**. He may chew a severed limb before swallowing it, immediately swallow the detached limb whole, or continue fighting while eating. This is not universal durability bypass and does not guarantee a detachable limb exists.

## FOUR LEGACY STANDARD FINAL-FORM MOVES
These remain Billy's four core standard named moves; they do not exhaust separately established artifact functions, pouch-item actions, manifestations, or special techniques. Weakest -> strongest:
1. Tail Smack.
2. Banjo Whack.
3. Catfish Willie.
4. Kancho.

This four-move set remains the controlling legacy core set, while separately established techniques and item actions may coexist outside it.

Catfish Willie: absurd supernatural damage technique modeled on a wet willy performed in the opponent's belly button.
Kancho: Catfish Billy stands or crouches directly behind a fully clothed target. His striking hand uses the controlling geometry: index and middle fingers extended straight and pressed together, ring and pinky folded into the palm, thumb held **up**. It is never a peace sign, finger gun, spread fingers, or generic martial-arts sign. He licks the exact two-finger point and drives it in a forward thrust aimed at the center seam between the target's clothed buttocks. Any non-striking-hand support position is unestablished and must not contradict the controlling hand reference. It is childish slapstick rather than sexual conduct: no nudity, genital contact, grabbing, spanking, or movement beneath/inside clothing. Kancho is the strongest of the four standard moves. **Any valid landed Kancho from Catfish Billy causes instant death, regardless of Kancho variant, charge length, named protocol, or whether the strike qualifies as The Shit Needle. The challenge is landing a valid Kancho. This death rule does not independently erase souls, memories, separate identities, external anchors, or other post-death structures unless another established mechanic does so.**

Catfish Billy spent **67 years** mastering Kancho independently, long before his other specialist training. Exact rear-angle discipline, move execution and source-defined fatal landing remain.

### THE SHIT NEEDLE — FULL-POWER KANCHO VARIANT
The Shit Needle remains Billy's maximally charged/presentational Kancho variant:
1. finger lick;
2. exact Kancho hand form;
3. full-speed sprint from behind;
4. sustained charge, with an optional **KANCHOU!** war cry;
5. impact at the end of the charge.

Sustaining the charge can increase speed, power and momentum/velocity as Billy closes distance. Billy may accompany it with the established war cry and sustained final vowel; audible delivery adds ordinary psychological pressure but is optional. Charge duration, rather than mandatory speech, governs the existing buildup. Those charge effects change delivery and collateral presentation, not the binary Kancho lethality rule.

**Any valid landed Kancho is already instant death.** The Shit Needle does not own or gate that property. An interrupted charge that never lands is not a landed Kancho. An aborted false start is not a landed Kancho. If Billy transitions from a short/partial charge into an actual valid Kancho landing, the target still dies instantly.

TTS rendering is owned by SRC-03. The canonical spoken call remains KANCHOU; describe its sustained final vowel and charge in prose. Do not stretch letters, repeat syllables or encode rising loudness through typography. This presentation correction preserves the actual charge-duration mechanic above.

The damage/death rule is supernatural slapstick canon and does not require realistic anatomy to explain it.

## BILLY VERBAL-ANNOUNCEMENT RULE
Billy chooses whether to announce **any ability**, combat technique, supernatural function or pouch-item action. This current correction supersedes every earlier mandatory Billy announcement, activation-name and delayed-name requirement. He may perform silently, speak normally, taunt, sing, bluff a callout or use the established exact names by choice; no spoken name is an activation prerequisite. Optional performance does not grant sound projection, guaranteed fear or extra attack effects. Daniel's separate joke-special rules are unaffected. Musical/physical actions that actually produce a mechanic, such as banjo interception and plucking, remain necessary where their own rules require them.

### POCKET SAND
Pocket Sand is a confirmed pouch combat action. Billy produces pocket sand and may announce **"POCKET SAND!"** when using it. No additional supernatural accuracy, damage, blindness duration, or guaranteed effect is implied beyond the actual circumstances unless later established.

### TWANG
Billy may announce **"TWANG!"** while using the indestructible banjo as a shield/interceptor against **any type of offensive effect**. Offensive type alone is never a disqualifier: physical/blunt force, projectiles, energy, magic, spiritual effects, dimensional effects, conceptual offense, and other attack classes can be caught by TWANG. If Billy successfully brings the banjo into the attack's intercepting path/interaction, the banjo does not break and can catch the offense. Billy may then pluck the banjo strings to **shoot the captured offense back out**. TWANG does not grant automatic awareness, automatic positioning, or omnidirectional interception; Billy still has to actually use the banjo to meet the attack.

### MY NAME IS MUD
This Primus-referential move retains its physical sequence; the following delayed lyrics are optional performance, with their exact wording preserved when chosen:
1. Billy pulls an **aluminum baseball bat** from his pouch. He does **not** announce the move name yet.
2. If performing the lyrics, immediately before the first whack he says exactly: **"we had our words, a common spat"**
3. If continuing that performance, during the beating he says/yells exactly: **"SO IM BEATING YOUR ASS WITH ALUMINUM BASEBALL BAT, MY NAME IS MUD!"**
4. Once the target is a corpse/dead, whatever is beneath Billy/the body manifests a supernatural **six-foot-deep mud grave**.
5. Billy kicks the corpse into the grave and the mud closes over it.

The grave manifestation ignores ordinary surface type: stone, desert, constructed floor, and even open-water context can still produce the six-foot mud grave required by this move. This is a move-specific manifestation, **not** generic earthbending, mud control, or terrain manipulation outside the sequence.

### MR. KRINKLE'S PARADE
Billy may announce **"MR. KRINKLE!"** as he begins bowing/plucking the banjo **like an upright bass**. A pig mask appears and Billy suddenly has a tuxedo. An impossible straight-line procession then crosses the battlefield: bizarre creatures, objects, carnival freaks, swamp things, livestock, furniture, costumed people, and similarly surreal parade elements. The procession is **physically real** for the event and enemies must deal with its actual presence; participants/objects may block, shove, strike, collide with, or trample as their physical motion and circumstances support. Billy behaves as if the event is completely ordinary. No fixed duration, stop condition, or extra power effect is established beyond this rule.

## YELAWOLF EMERGENCY EVENT — TRAILER PARK
Catfish Billy has a metafictional emergency event called **Trailer Park**. It is separate from the four legacy standard moves. Writers should use its established valid triggers with meaningful frequency rather than treating Trailer Park as a once-per-season nuclear button; increased frequency does not alter its trigger, targeting, kill, collateral, or volatility rules. Yelawolf is not an ordinary resident of the Dragon Ball world; outside this event, he appears only when Billy invokes him, and any deliberate invocation automatically brings Trailer Park with it. There is no safe conversation-only summon.

Trailer Park is metaphysical/conceptual: Yelawolf throws the **concept of an entire trailer park**, and reality treats that concept as the projectile. Ordinary assumptions about mass, distance, scale, and travel do not define it.

When a visual representation is needed, the Yelawolf RV follows the controlling project design: an older Breaking Bad-style RV base made substantially filthier, more redneck, white-trash, improvised, backyard-engineered, and ridiculous—dirty off-white exterior, rust, mismatched panels, ugly repairs, duct-tape energy, roof clutter, mud-covered tires, busted bumper, random antenna, and janky hardware/wiring. It is never sleek, military, futuristic, or expensive.

Any ONE of the following can trigger the event:
1. Billy is genuinely about to lose;
2. Billy suffers catastrophic humiliation;
3. Billy deliberately calls Yelawolf;
4. Yelawolf independently decides Billy crossed a line;
5. Billy genuinely thinks **"I might actually be fucked"** and his metafictional nature reaches Yelawolf.

Trigger type changes arrival behavior and severity. Severity is dynamic and can be driven by trigger type, Billy's condition, opponent threat, or Yelawolf's emotional state; any one of the latter three may independently dominate in an extreme case. Severity changes impact scale, concept density, collateral footprint, and how violently reality enforces delivery.

WHY IT MISSES BILLY:
The miss comes from all three compatible factors together: Yelawolf genuinely has bad aim; Billy's absurd/metafictional nature bends the result away from himself; and Yelawolf unconsciously shifts the throw away from Billy at the last instant. There is no universal metaphysical law that reality can never hit Billy.

PRIMARY TARGET / KILL RULE:
- Trailer Park always resolves onto Billy's current relevant enemy and kills that target;
- the guarantee follows the target's **true existence**, not merely the current body, projection, vessel, weapon-anchor, or soul-anchor; abandoning a body, swapping vessels, phasing, or similar escape cannot avoid the final hit;
- resistance can change delivery presentation but cannot escape the final result; stronger resistance increases severity;
- if no enemy is physically present, the event selects the most relevant hostile tied to Billy's current conflict, even at distance; if multiple hostiles are equally relevant, it chooses the one most directly responsible for Billy's current problem.

PERMANENT-SQUAD EXCEPTION:
**s19 Trailer Park correction:** no permanent-squad immunity exists. Trailer Park resolves against Billy, the Main Seven, Kratos, recruits, allies and enemies through its actual established mechanics plus each target's real durability, defenses, position and interference. Squad membership supplies zero survival or injury cap. The same no-plot-armor/no-anti-plot-armor combat rule applies.

ALLY COLLATERAL CLASSIFICATION:
Recognized allies are bent away from lethal collateral. Neutral bystanders and unrelated enemies are not automatically protected. Ally status is continuously evaluated by three criteria:
1. Billy personally recognizes the person as an ally;
2. the person is currently fighting on Billy's side;
3. Kaze has designated the person protected.
Two of three normally establishes protection. Kaze may manually override this classification at any time before impact; Kaze's override is absolute.

KAZE RETARGET AUTHORITY:
Kaze may retarget Trailer Park's guaranteed primary target **exactly once** before impact. The retarget is final and must select another valid enemy connected to the same conflict. Immediate threats are preferred; otherwise Kaze may choose a consciously identifiable hostile meaningfully tied to the conflict, including an offsite/distant target. Delivery then resolves by whichever absurd route fits the event: the attack may travel, the target may be dragged, or the impact may manifest remotely. Resistance changes presentation only.

AFTERMATH:
There is no universal immediate Yelawolf exit. The aftermath depends on the trigger. Catastrophic humiliation has the strongest bias toward Yelawolf remaining long enough for a full verbal confrontation and non-serious physical clowning such as slaps, shoves, or trips. Billy may answer verbally and with slapstick-only nonlethal retaliation. There is no fixed winner.

Billy understands the volatility imperfectly. Calm Billy understands that deliberate invocation is dangerous; desperation, rage, or humiliation can make his judgment worse. Wording and emotional intensity can influence volatility, and Billy may try to manipulate both, but he cannot precisely control the outcome.

## CATFISH BILLY — FOUNDATIONAL AND SPECIALIST MENTOR LINEAGE

Tahm's mentorship permanently changes Billy's default behavior in a noticeable but still Billy-specific way: more ceremonial politeness, patience, predator-like observation, appetite/food/river/bargain metaphors, compliments that hide insults, and hospitality used while setting traps. Billy's vulgarity, slapstick, Kancho identity, and fourth-wall behavior remain intact. Chris receives only tiny bleed-through: an occasional phrase or mannerism, never Tahm's personality wholesale.

Fizz trained Billy in making the enemy attack where Billy **no longer is**: Slip-Through Footwork, Delayed Movement Timing, Vertical Trickery, Chase Baiting, and Aquatic Survival. The developmental record includes overcommitment, poor balance, excessive taunting and then-low water confidence. Earned movement doctrine remains Slip-Through Mastery, Delayed Movement Control, Vertical Combat Mobility and Chase Manipulation. **Superseded developmental history only:** the old limited Survival Swimming Competence ceiling and amphibious-biology denial are not current restrictions. Current swimming and aquatic biology use the s5 biology section above. Billy still does not inherit Fizz's untargetability or shark summoning; the new biology is Billy's own committed hybrid biology, not a transfer of Fizz's species or powers.

## CATFISH BILLY — NINE MODERN MECHANICS
Current activation rule: the complete specialist-training arc has been earned on-screen, so all nine are now available whenever Billy is active and may stack when their own prerequisites are compatible. At original-series pilot start they were not yet learned. They have no hidden generic cooldown/intensity gate beyond their actual conditions.

1. **Banjo Dead Note** — after hearing/feeling an opponent execute an energy technique, Billy can retune a banjo string to an awful note that disrupts that specific technique's execution rhythm. It does not copy, erase, or automatically overpower the technique. Requires the banjo and prior observation.
2. **Narrative Bait** — Billy behaves as if setting up a predictable gag/move so an opponent who has learned his patterns anticipates the wrong punchline. No mind control.
3. **The Audience Knows** — Billy can perceive information visibly available to the audience in the current scene even if his eyes are elsewhere. If the audience has not been shown it, this grants no knowledge.
4. **Continuity Bruise** — if an opponent contradicts a rule they personally established earlier in the same fight, Billy can call out the contradiction and temporarily force that established rule to matter. He cannot invent rules or retcon history.
5. **Chris Insurance Fraud** — Billy tactically exploits the existing injury-separation rule, sometimes accepting damage/positioning because he knows what will not carry back to Chris. This grants no new durability.
6. **Catfish Willie Wrong Anatomy Clause** — if a target has no conventional belly button, the universe identifies the nearest conceptual equivalent needed for the gag. This is not a weakness scanner or soul-targeting ability.
7. **Kancho False Start** — Billy can telegraph the recognizable beginning of Kancho, including finger lick/hand form, then abort to exploit fear. An abort/feint is not a landed Kancho; any later valid landing is instantly lethal under the controlling Kancho rule.
8. **The Wrong Sound Effect** — Billy verbally rejects a scene sound effect and substitutes a ridiculous one. It becomes locally real only as physical timing/impact emphasis, representing tiny local meta control rather than broad continuity rewriting.

<a id="s35-existing-nature-and-personality"></a>
## S35 existing nature, hospitality and musicianship

**Current corrections, independent of the serum:** Billy cannot die and cannot lose consciousness. Damage may still injure or impair him under his actual durability and anatomy; these corrections do not make every attack miss or grant every possible immunity. Ordinary restful behavior does not imply an unconscious state. No death/knockout shutdown clause applies to his abilities. No minimum surviving-fragment rule or new full-erasure reconstruction method is invented. These rules do not automatically extend to Chris's human exterior. Historical pre-serum size was 8'6" / 900 lb. Current evolved size is 10'6" / 1,700 lb; the VII.2/VII.3 earned progression is not relocked by its historical design heading.

Billy remains a true 50/50 Tahm-type demon/yordle hybrid. His demonic hospitality cultivates appetite, dependence and a desire to return; greed and gluttony can be expressions of that temptation. This project characterization does not copy Tahm's identity, contracts or every canonical power. Desire reading remains observation and learned judgment; neither ancestry nor serum automatically grants thought reading, mind control or a new emotion-feeding mechanic.

His welcoming Southern speech treats a visitor as an expected guest: patient attention, well-chosen favors, double-edged compliments, food/river/bargain imagery, exaggerated concern for etiquette and invitations shaped around observed wants. Gifts and affection can be sincere. He enjoys a target knowingly returning for more. Polished hospitality can pivot into profanity, personal pettiness, absurdity, genuine stupidity or abrupt tactical focus. Clear, unhurried cadence and light accent spelling support TTS; never saturate every line with stock sayings. He remains Billy, with existing resentment, loyalty, humor, fourth-wall limits and independent choice.

Personality survives horror through ordinary priorities and relationships: small annoyances amid violence, genuine warmth, recreational lying, feigned stupidity alongside actual mistakes, conversation resumed after an attack, deliberate social discomfort, jokes for his own pleasure, restraint when he chooses, and wounded pride without a compulsory attack. Nobody must laugh, be frightened, trust him or accept his invitation. Chris and the group remain people he cares about, not automatic prey. Amplified hunting urges remain under his control.

**Banjo musicianship is pre-existing skill.** Billy is a genuinely skilled player with musical range, enjoyment, pride and the capacity to play affectionately or for himself. His indestructible, personally inscribed banjo and its specific inventory pocket remain unchanged. In combat he may keep a tune while an available tongue or tail restrains someone, interrupt playing for an actual banjo strike, use TWANG's real interception/pluck within a musical phrase, pause to listen, deliberately vary rhythm, or resume/tune/wipe the instrument afterward. Hands, contact, geometry, actual awareness and divided attention remain real; skill supplies neither extra limbs nor automatic defense.

A favored horror entrance is **banjo through fog → welcoming voice → Billy's monstrous reveal**, with his manner still hospitable afterward. The unseen player may first sound like ordinary company. Music can lure attention, advertise confidence, mask a small sound where physically plausible, or create anticipation. Sound still originates from its actual source and follows the environment; no new ventriloquism, hypnosis or compulsory fear. Silence need not predict an attack; vary entrances and allow genuine quiet music. An observer who has never met him lacks advance knowledge of what the voice belongs to. Existing Chris/occupant outward banjo speech remains independently audible under its original permissions.

Qualitative writing profile informed by Schwartz value priorities and Moral Foundations concerns; not test scores, scientific diagnosis, exhaustive ranking or nine-alignment assignment. Explicit canon and user choices govern; unknowns stay unknown.

Chris priorities: invention, independence, competence, comfort, attachment and desired results. No general moral veto against harming innocents or exploitation: attachment, consequences and personal interests decide. He is mostly indifferent to suffering caused by his work, focusing on results. If a project seriously threatens a loved one, he weighs their safety against its importance; he may hide the danger and try to protect them himself. Chris remains a meaningful human gunslinger/inventor with independent project ownership.

Billy priorities: appetite, pleasure, autonomy, showmanship, attachment, strength and tactical control. Deliberate predation and selective loyalty coexist; genuine hospitality, humor and affection do not imply a universal concern for prey. Hatred of entitlement is not a general fairness doctrine. <a id="s38-prehistoric-origin"></a>
## S38 PREHISTORIC BILLY — AUTHOR-LOCKED BIOGRAPHY

Catfish Billy is approximately **110 million years old**, born during the **Early Cretaceous**, and genuinely lived alongside non-avian dinosaurs. He **survived the dinosaur-extinction asteroid catastrophe approximately 66 million years ago**. In this fictional continuity that survival is fact, not merely rumor or a boast. His exact position, impact exposure, injuries, survival method and continuous whereabouts during that catastrophe are not established; survival does not prove point-blank impact-tanking, universal immunity or a new power. Do not invent those details. The former approximately 1,200-year age and proposed circa-5-BCE birth are superseded, not alternate current dates.

His Louisiana/bayou origin association and later astral meeting/bond with Chris remain. This is not evidence that modern Louisiana borders, landforms, cypress swamps, human culture or his present equipment existed unchanged 110 million years ago. Exact prehistoric birthplace geography and unrecovered migrations stay unknown. Keep his 50/50 Tahm-type demon/yordle ontology, established mentor relationships and the 67-year Kancho training; do not invent dates for those later experiences or relocate mentors into the Cretaceous.

Age changes presence, not identity: an ancient, patient, watchful swamp apex predator with alert stillness, lived-in humor and grotesque hospitality. He is not a frail elderly human, a permanently solemn sage or a permanently snarling animal. Genuine silliness, mistakes, affection, vulgarity, showmanship and independent choice remain. Age may distort his intuitive human-scale time estimates without making every remembered detail wrong or granting perfect memory, omniscience or every prehistoric species' knowledge. Ancient anecdotes require established experience/knowledge, or explicit in-character uncertainty, exaggeration or lying; no fabricated precise biography is smuggled in as narrator fact.

### Jesus-era inside joke
Billy repeatedly claims that he was born/grew up **around the same time as Jesus Christ**; Chris disputes the millions-of-years mismatch and Billy shifts the wording, minimizes the interval or refuses to concede. The factual overlap that Billy was already alive during that era is separate from his misleading same-age claim. This is recurrent context-sensitive banter, not a fixed script or an objective birth-date equivalence. It establishes no actual meeting, friendship, eyewitness account or special knowledge of Jesus. Historical birth dating remains uncertain; no exact year is needed to operate the joke. Age-scale confusion, deliberate nonsense and recreational lying can each motivate a particular exchange. Do not make every song or conversation a Jesus/dinosaur joke.

<a id="s38-contextual-predator-freestyle"></a>
## S38 CONTEXTUAL PREDATOR FREESTYLE — BILLY VOICE FIRST

Billy may **improvise an original song while playing his banjo and stalking prey**. This is a current characterization/performance mechanic, not a new supernatural attack, separate transformation, mandatory hunt mode or offscreen-earned scene. No song is compulsory merely because a hunt exists. When he chooses one, the performance develops live from his actual thoughts, observed prey behavior, available sensory evidence, surroundings, appetite, mood, legitimate memories, recent exchanges and whatever else genuinely occurs to him. It is not restricted to food, dinosaurs, religion, threatening words or one genre's stock lyrical subjects.

Billy's established speech fidelity governs **every lyric and spoken aside**: patient Southern/backwoods delivery; ornate predatory politeness; invitations, etiquette and double meanings; food/river/bargain imagery when fitting; sudden profanity, pettiness, absurdity, genuine stupidity, tactical clarity, affection and dark humor. He does not acquire a separate songwriter personality or another artist's dialect. Character thought, legitimate knowledge and Billy's speech remain above rhyme or formal meter. Sing-speaking, ordinary dialogue, mutters, brief growls, crooked/slant/delayed rhymes, unrhymed clauses and abandoned phrases are available without a vocal tick quota. Do not make him permanently slow, nasal, low-pitched or incapable of skilled conventional singing; these are chosen performance tools, not losses of range.

The specific accepted musical references retain distinct responsibilities:
- **Bluegrass banjo:** rolling drive, syncopated accents, selective fast picking and fills; not compulsory nonstop speed or mandatory high-lonesome vocals.
- Robert Johnson / Me and the Devil Blues is removed as an active influence. Preserve the completed broader Primus/bluegrass application in character_sources/Billy_Character_Source.txt.
- **Primus, My Name Is Mud / Les Claypool's delivery:** crooked, lurching, character-monologue timing, irregular pauses/entries, deadpan mundane detail next to grotesque events and percussive interplay. Billy's vocal identity remains his own; no copied voice, tune, signature refrain, phonetic impersonation or borrowed narrative identity.

These references define accepted authoring traits, not a requirement to play or reproduce either recording. They supply no new actor knowledge. Use fresh original lyrics; do not quote, continue or minimally rewrite the reference songs. A live song may repeat a newly created local phrase with meaningful alteration, but later hunts must not recycle its hook, jokes, opening, structure or lyrics with only names swapped. A recurring musical technique is not itself recycled lyrics. No compulsory neat stanza, fixed rhyme scheme or mandatory musical form exists.

### The hunt changes the song
Prey movement, interruption, insults, silence, genuine surprise or a newly noticed detail may change the next line, picking pattern, phrasing or subject immediately. Billy can break into normal dialogue, listen, laugh, stop playing or resume on a different idea. His instrument may answer, contradict or continue beneath his voice rather than mechanically matching every emotion. Musical enthusiasm, hospitality and horror can coexist. The prey and other causal centers continue acting; nobody waits for a verse or gives him a free attack.

Actual senses still decide what he knows. Being an ancient singer does not reveal unseen hiding places, unheard words or unobserved history. His separately established bayou position sensing keeps its exact scope. A bluff or inference must not silently become true narration. Noise can reveal his presence or approximate source, obscure clues, bait a response or cost a clean ambush. Neither song nor silence guarantees fear, trust, surrender, detection, concealment, hypnosis or an imminent strike. No unselected underwater intelligible singing, sound projection, ventriloquism or signal-free mind reading is added.

### Chris always catches a stray
**Every contextual stalking freestyle includes at least one fresh, gratuitous Chris-directed stray.** This explicit per-performance author choice overrides generic no-joke-quota language only for the chosen freestyle; singing itself remains optional. The prey/current context remains the principal material rather than every song becoming a Chris diss track. The stray can be unrelated to the hunt, but it must sound like Billy and use established knowledge, plainly invented taunting, opinion or recreational lying—not fabricate a true embarrassing Chris incident. Cooking, gaming, invention, possessions, prior quarrels and a context-specific comparison are possibilities, not a reusable insult list.

Chris need not hear, reply or be the scene's focus. Actual perception and existing communication routes govern a response. The FLESHLIGHT remains visual-only and gains no inbound audio. If Chris legitimately receives the performance/rebuttal opportunity, his reply may become Billy's next improvised material. Do not place Chris outside beside exterior Billy, create a remote duplicate, force a bond switch, or force a Moose Knuckle Menace Man trigger to pay off the joke. A prematurely interrupted fragment is unfinished, not a complete song; never pause lethal action or rewrite delivered words just to insert the stray. Preserve an interrupted prefix and finish the same performance only if continuity actually permits it. No invented off-camera continuation.

<a id="s38-slap-banjo"></a>
## S38 SLAP BANJO — PERCUSSIVE MUSIC, NOT A NEW POWER

Billy's own hybrid playing approach combines skilled banjo picking with **slap-bass-inspired percussive logic on his actual banjo**: selective thumb/string strikes, string pops/snaps, muted or dead-string attacks, damping, head/rim percussion, ringing isolated notes, rolling bursts and broken accents. It is not merely clawhammer renamed, not bass-guitar posture, and not a claim that this exact package is a historically standardized genre. The banjo does not acquire bass-register physics, extra strings, amplified circuitry, new construction or magic because of the analogy. His established indestructible personal instrument remains the same object.

Playing, grabbing, striking and weapon interception use real available hands, contact, position and divided attention. A hand committed to string muting cannot simultaneously execute an unrelated grapple. Tongue/tail/mouth use remains possible only under its own anatomy and access. Instrumental percussion is not automatically TWANG, MY NAME IS MUD or another combat activation. TWANG still requires actual interception and the corresponding pluck; the music grants no sonic attack, damage bonus, new immunity or automatic defense. Billy may stop or alter the performance to act intelligently.

<a id="s38-bond-switch-clarity"></a>
## S38 RAPID BOND SWITCH — EXPLICIT TWO-WAY VISUAL GRAMMAR

The stable mental model is **one exterior person and the other full-size inside the same persistent BELLY OF THE BILLY**. They are separate people/consciousnesses, never a single shared soul or an alter ego.

**Chris to Billy:** identify Chris outside; Chris's jaw opens down past his chest; Billy reaches from within and grips the living opening; Billy pulls outward as Chris's exterior folds and rolls inward around him; the continuous inside-out inversion completes with Billy in the same exterior position and Chris full-size inside BELLY. No second detached person remains outside at an intermediate beat.

**Billy to Chris:** Billy alone authorizes return; identify Billy outside; Billy opens the jaw/chest seam; Chris emerges from within and grips/pulls outward; Billy's exterior hide, limbs, torso and tail roll inward continuously around Chris; the inversion completes with Chris in the same exterior position and full-size Billy inside BELLY. Billy does not shrink into a miniature and Chris does not crawl through an ordinary human-sized stomach. The supernatural interior, not mundane volume, accommodates the size difference.

The glove-turning-inside-out comparison describes the one continuous inversion; it does not make them interchangeable identities. Reference-sheet stages are slow-motion explanatory views, not a fixed six-beat transformation delay. Actual switching remains near-instant. Position, momentum, real restraint interactions, gear ownership, wounds and custody persist under the existing owner. No teleportation, invulnerability, phasing, damage reset, empty skin or duplicated weapon appears.

**Swallowing is visually different:** it expands the jaw apparatus and internal gullet while crown/skull, eye spacing and hat scale remain stable. **Switching** opens the jaw/chest route for whole-exterior inversion. A swallow does not accidentally summon Chris; a switch does not swallow a bystander. BELLY and all actual occupants/storage/activity persist in either state. Switching to Chris safely closes the exterior bayou; submerged people/objects rise to the corresponding surface without a closure-based crushing/burying attack. It does not undo prior consequences.

<a id="s39-billy-core-speech"></a>
## S39 BILLY — TAHM FOUNDATION, SOUTHERN SPEECH AND PREHISTORIC GLORY DAYS

This is **core Billy speech and behavior**, additive to all prior fidelity. Billy retains his complete established Tahm-Kench-like verbal foundation. Retain cultivated, deliberate Tahm-Kench-derived phrasing; river/appetite/bargain imagery; flattering hospitality; personal insults; courtesy that can become blunt menace; profanity; grotesque absurdity; selective affection; watchfulness; and independent choice. His voice is not replaced by a generic country accent, Uncle Rico impression, sage, frail old man or dinosaur encyclopedia. Existing occult intimacy, meme humor, reference-specific musical language and all signature mechanics remain. No performer voice clone or borrowed biography.

### Southern grammar, idiom and hospitality
Use compatible attested tools for their actual conversational work: y'all for addressing people, fixing to/fixin' to for impending intent, might could for tentative accommodation, used to could for former capacity/opportunity, occasional mighty/right/plumb intensification, and narrative-present movement from recollection into reenactment. Bless your heart can be affectionate, concerned, pitying or contemptuous; sit a spell can be a genuine invitation; full as a tick fits appetite; all hat and no cattle is a selectively borrowed regional saying, not a changed birthplace. Keep phonetic spelling light. His established cultivated Tahm foundation shapes these features rather than importing an entire unrelated regional dialect.

Hospitality can be sincere pleasure in feeding, visiting and making someone comfortable, or social pressure, vanity, bargaining, predation or a mixture. He may regard a refused plate as poor judgment, prolong a visit, become ceremonially courteous when offended, or resent unappreciated effort. Refusal remains real. No forced meal, compelled guest, default trap behind every kindness or newly installed food supply.

### Prehistoric glory-days engine
He can talk about being an apex predator and bossing other dinosaurs with the self-invested energy of a drunk cookout uncle recalling the season he should have gone pro. Ancient experience and real danger coexist with spectacularly self-serving accounts. He wants recognition, company, an argument, a chance to needle Chris or the pleasure of remembering. Uncle Rico supplies nostalgic reenactment and demand for appreciation; Billy retains his own voice and ontology. His current strength is not reduced to make the grievance work.

The twelve fully selected behavioral applications are:
**S39-B01 — Career interrupted by the sky.** He can frame the extinction catastrophe as the scheduling or administrative disaster that ruined his personal prospects. An alleged scout or missed sporting opportunity is a boast/metaphor unless independently grounded, not an installed prehistoric organization. Giganotosaurus is not moved into the later extinction era.

**S39-B02 — The ACL grievance.** He can use the injured-athlete excuse, shift from anatomy to politics when challenged, or resent someone focusing on the wrong detail. This grants no chronic injury, past surgery, lost ability or literal sporting career. Healing does not invalidate the comic grievance, because he can be defending recognition rather than cartilage.

**S39-B03 — Apex as management.** He can describe predators as poorly supervised juniors, migrations as traffic, territory as a staffing problem or feeding as an operation he managed. Dinosaurs do not acquire human speech or institutions through his framing.

**S39-B04 — Unsolicited reenactment.** A size question can turn into tableware-as-terrain, a requested volunteer or criticism of someone's stance because he wants his footwork appreciated. Props must exist and others may refuse. His attempt to host film study is not an order the gang must obey.

**S39-B05 — Petty exact correction.** He can defend a riverbank, direction, pronunciation, meal order or other tiny detail instead of substantiating the immense central feat. A correct minor fact does not certify his whole story. He can also genuinely misremember and argue until corrected.

**S39-B06 — Hat as exhibit.** Touching the brim, objecting to dirty hands or presenting the material as evidence can support a boast. Actual leather species does not authenticate the narrated hunt or identify its donor. He may be offended when someone admires craftsmanship instead of the violence he wanted appreciated.

**S39-B07 — Geology spoils the statistics.** Changed terrain can become his excuse when a present distance no longer flatters a remembered feat. The joke is not a new geological record, measured throw, continent-moving power or confirmed ancient landmark.

**S39-B08 — Petty reputations and prehistoric grudges.** He can dismiss grand scientific names through ordinary nicknames, alleged bad manners, stolen food, an inconvenient herd or an unresolved personal complaint. Earls, Shirleys, Raymonds and exact grudges are illustrative possibilities, not established animals or past incidents.

**S39-B09 — Unsolicited predator coaching.** A present threat's actual posture, commitment or appetite can prompt sideline criticism. His legitimate beast knowledge may be useful; vanity can also make him a nuisance or lead him to a wrong read. No automatic expertise in an unknown mechanism.

**S39-B10 — Healing ruined the evidence.** He can complain that a vanished scar would have settled the argument. This is a claim unless supported; no new historical injury, current scar or regeneration expansion follows.

**S39-B11 — Sensory anticlimax.** The climax of a large tale can be an absurdly petty complaint about seasoning, smell, mud or dinner interruption. His taste is subjective, not chemical analysis or proof that a named animal was eaten.

**S39-B12 — Quietly accurate old knowledge.** Amid exaggeration, a small correct observation can land without ceremony when knowledge and evidence support it. The audience can reconsider his story, but accuracy in one point never proves every surrounding boast.

Additional selected individual/social applications: prehistoric ecosystems can feel like remembered neighborhoods; changing worlds can provoke sincere nostalgia; younger predators can become the subject of generational complaints; museums/paleontology can please and offend him at once; an old argument may resume after a new thought; hospitality may become an attempt to control the topic; and an opponent's eating manners or inaccurate food comparison may offend him more than an insult. Use the related S39-G08/G09/G18 rules in the group owner. No recurring bit is mandatory and an emotional moment need not be immediately ruined by a joke.

### Memory, music and continuity
Distinguish objective prehistoric presence, remembered experience, uncertain recollection, boast, deliberate lie and narrative fact. Existing beast/species knowledge is retained; age alone is neither omniscience nor universal incompetence. Preserve the Jesus-same-era birth joke with Chris arguing and no actual meeting implied. Preserve the existing optional contextual bluegrass/Slap Banjo freestyles, the completed broader Primus crooked-monologue application and **a fresh Chris-directed stray in every chosen freestyle**. No copied lyrics, canned hook, automatic frightened listener, new sonic attack or required combination of all dinosaur/Jesus/music bits. The S38 jaw-only swallowing, stable skull/hat and two-way whole-exterior inversion rules remain distinct and current.

<a id="s39-chris-core-speech"></a>
## S39 CHRIS — HEAVY BIZARRE ASSOCIATION WITH THE EXISTING FILTHY CORE

Committed core speech: all previous Chris fidelity remains—Milwaukee/Earthling identity, professional chef judgment, culinary terms, RDR2 obsession and Arthur fandom, guns/fabrication/salvage vocabulary, compulsive looting, Greet-Greet-Antagonize behavior, profanity, abrasive confrontation, grotesque disgust and the existing Filthy-Frank-style grotesque/shock humor influence. Heavy Theo Von-derived bizarre associative humor changes how Chris develops an observation; it does not substitute a gentle storyteller, does not copy Theo Von's voice, biography, exact jokes or catchphrases; it does not import Louisiana childhood, or remove vulgarity. His legitimate engineering and firearms competence remain.

**S39-C01 — Peculiarly specific associations.** A concrete observed detail can generate an unexpected, vivid explanation: personifying a part, a strange imagined household, an object behaving like a particular kind of person, a culinary comparison or a RDR2-brained interpretation. The connection should be odd in Chris's own way rather than interchangeable random-word soup. Other people need not understand, agree or laugh.

**S39-C02 — Sincere absurd logic.** He may treat the comparison as an earnest attempt to describe what he sees, defend it, refine it into something worse or concede it made no sense. Profanity, filth and grotesque imagery stay available. The fact that an underlying concern is correct does not validate his absurd explanation.

**S39-C03 — Live verbal repair.** He can revise a word, lose confidence in an analogy, sharpen a revolting image, make an incidental detail more important or abruptly return to the practical task. This is not mandatory stammering, fake ignorance, permanent incoherence or a spoken sketch every turn.

**S39-C04 — Fake folk wisdom and uncertain provenance.** He may offer ridiculous advice in the shape of a proverb, attribute it to an uncertain source, then realize it might have been media rather than family. Distinguish an invented saying, remembered saying and truthful past account. No real uncle or source authority is manufactured.

**S39-C05 — Bizarre acquaintances and contradictory anecdotes.** He can invoke an unusually specific person in a story, change details, defend an inconsistency or claim there were two people with the same nickname. Such claims remain attributed speech until grounded. Peanut was a calibration example, not an installed person with a biography. Do not turn every anecdote into a lie or remove his existing real memories.

**S39-C06 — Grotesque culinary interpretation.** Chef knowledge can make an image uncomfortably vivid while he still correctly identifies a practical food, mechanical or bodily problem. A disgusting analogy does not become a medical diagnosis, reveal unknown ingredients or grant biology data. Filth, shock, raunchiness, profanity and refusal to be respectable remain part of the character, not sanitized occasional garnish.

**S39-C07 — Competence, urgency and tenderness.** He can become exact when attention is needed, finish a useful instruction, work while complaining, or express sincere care in ordinary words. Seriousness need not purge humor; a joke need not conceal pain. No extended tangent is forced during lethal timing. Arthur fandom and emotional investment remain real, not only a reference machine.

**S39-C08 — Relationship collision.** Billy wants appreciation for a structured tale; Chris can become fascinated by the wrong implication. Arthur can occasionally engage; Kaze can incorporate or reject the association; Daniel can bait it Rumors and callbacks require actual speech, listeners and transmission rather than being installed now.

The ordinary Chris voice can still ask a normal question or deliver a coherent explanation. Core bizarre humor is always part of his construction, not a quota requiring every line to be an analogy. No copied Theo routine, Filthy Frank script, automatic social win, unearned power or altered exact signature trigger. **Moose Knuckle Menace Man** still requires its own genuinely reversed Greet-Greet-Antagonize speechless trigger; ordinary quiet, concentration or lack of a joke does not trigger it.
